- **svcc23-eval**: Evaluates singing voice conversion systems on in-domain (singing-to-singing) and cross-domain (speech-to-singing) speaker conversion. It probes the model's ability to preserve target speaker identity and musical prosody while converting source audio to the target voice. Use when the user wants to benchmark on SVCC 2023, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports perceptual quality (subjective evaluation). ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/svcc23-eval) · ★3)
- **svcd-eval**: This protocol evaluates a singing voice conversion model's ability to transform a source singer's voice into a target singer's timbre while preserving musical naturalness and pitch accuracy. It measures both subjective perceptual quality and objective acoustic fidelity using human ratings and signal processing metrics. Use when the user wants to benchmark on VCTK, NUS-48E, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports MOS (Naturalness), MOS (Similarity). ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/svcd-eval) · ★3)
- **SVC指令追踪**: SVC 系统调用指令追踪与分析（供安全研究/自有应用）。当 App 用内联汇编直接发 svc 指令绕过 libc 标准库函数（导致 Frida hook libc 失效）时，用于识别 svc 指令、解析 syscall 编号、还原它在做什么系统调用。涵盖 arm64 svc 机制、syscall 号对照、追踪手段。 ([Forinxy/-skills](https://github.com/Forinxy/-skills/tree/HEAD/Android%E9%80%86%E5%90%91%E5%B7%A5%E7%A8%8B%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E5%90%88%E9%9B%86/SVC%E6%8C%87%E4%BB%A4%E8%BF%BD%E8%B8%AA) · ★52 · 2 copies)

- **svd-based-pca-module-projection**: Use when a normalized gene expression matrix (genes as rows, samples as columns) must be reduced to per-gene-set activity scores with statistical significance, distinguishing shifted from overdispersed modules. - **svd-based-principal-component-extraction**: Use when analyzing a scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g., Lawlor alpha cells, 101 cells) and you need the singular vectors/PCs — e. - **svd-low-rank-approximation**: Use when a normalized scRNA-seq count matrix (cells as rows, genes as columns) contains a large fraction of zeros (e.g. &gt;90% with droplet-based data) and you need a low-rank approximation to complete technical zeros before adaptive thresholding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/svd-low-rank-approximation))
